# All guitar and acoustic-guitar multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

All 137 recorded guitar/acoustic **multi-effects**, including effects-only HX Effects and Cerberus. Amp modeling, an expression pedal and floor operation are not inclusion requirements. Dedicated single-function pedals are in [SINGLE-GUITAR.md](SINGLE-GUITAR.md), outside these tables and active counts.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

USB/DGT Out Lev jointly controls USB and DIGITAL OUT level; USB Mix Level mixes computer audio with Guitar1–3 input selection. — Evidence: [Manual pp90,92,95](https://manualzilla.com/doc/7429915/boss-gt-10-owner-s-manual)<br>**USB / editor / platforms:** USB audio/MIDI documented. System Program1.02 is an updater, not an editor. — Evidence: [Support listing](https://www.boss.info/fi/products/gt-10/)<br>**Editor / Windows restrictions:** Librarian1.00 manages user-patch collections; official page lists Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10. Its reference to an Editor does not identify or verify a separate full editor. Driver1.0.0 supports Windows10 32/64-bit, excludes Mobile, virtual Windows and Windows on Mac; USB3 ports must support USB2. Roland support updated2025-05-19 explicitly says **Windows11 unsupported**. This concerns the GT-10 driver, not proof every standard-audio mode fails. — Evidence: [Librarian](https://in.boss.info/support/by_product/gt-10/updates_drivers/2938); [Driver](https://in.boss.info/support/by_product/gt-10/updates_drivers/351091); [Windows support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/115003126763-GT-10-Windows-10-Driver-Installation-and-Troubleshooting-Instructions)<br>**USB audio / recording modes:** Standard mode uses the OS driver but **cannot use USB MIDI**; MIDI requires Advanced mode and the dedicated driver. Changing driver mode requires restart. Official support specifies a USB A–B cable, establishing USB-B on the unit. Direct Monitor controls GT-10 sound at OUTPUT/PHONES, resets On at power-up and can be controlled by ASIO2 software with the special driver. Off requires computer monitoring to hear the guitar. — Evidence: [Indexed manual pp91,93](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-10_OM.pdf); [Windows support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/115003126763-GT-10-Windows-10-Driver-Installation-and-Troubleshooting-Instructions) | **Routing / live limits:** Preamp Single, Dual Mono, Dual L/R or Dynamic; Dynamic switches channels by input level. Dual modes allow0–100ms channel-B delay. Main delay1–3400ms; dual delays each1–1700ms. Rapid playing after a dual-delay patch change can miss the intended initial effect; downstream mono FX/COSM cancels stereo delay. — Evidence: [Manual pp98,119–120](https://manualzilla.com/doc/7429915/boss-gt-10-owner-s-manual)<br>**USB recording / MIDI routing:** USB connection disables MIDI transfer through DIN connectors. USB In selects computer playback for processing/re-recording; disable software thru to prevent recirculation. USB/DGT Out Lev jointly controls USB and DIGITAL OUT level; USB Mix Level mixes computer audio with Guitar1–3 input selection. — Evidence: [Manual pp90,92,95](https://manualzilla.com/doc/7429915/boss-gt-10-owner-s-manual)<br>**Firmware / live restrictions:** System Program **1.02** fixes patch-change sound loss/abnormal noise, GRAPHIC EQ acting despite being off, and recalled effect states becoming inconsistent during rapid patch changes when Smooth mode or PHRASE LOOP is active. Also fixes FX CHAIN meter cursor tracking; improves chorus-mode transitions and category-keyword refresh. These are version-specific fixes, not proof of universal gapless switching. — Evidence: [Japanese release notes](https://www.boss.info/jp/support/by_product/gt-10/updates_drivers/da43b806-9a00-4ed8-a3a1-57413d576db0/)<br>**Firmware / memory maintenance:** Update preserves user memory; BOSS recommends Librarian or bulk-dump backup. Transfer uses MIDI or USB with the dedicated driver and a SysEx-capable SMF utility; UpdSMF is an update transport, not a patch editor. — Evidence: [Japanese release notes](https://www.boss.info/jp/support/by_product/gt-10/updates_drivers/da43b806-9a00-4ed8-a3a1-57413d576db0/)<br>**Looper / drums / live:** Manufacturer Q&A confirms **38s mono /19s stereo**, overdubbing and **no saving the recorded performance**. This supports the workshop38s figure; Finnish product40s remains a conflicting regional claim. BANK pedals operate basic looping; additional assignments extend control. Manual p60 allows retaining bank switching by assigning loop control to CTL1–4; Clear Only erases immediately, while Mute/Clear first mutes and a second press erases. Drums/scenes/trails unverified. — Evidence: [Q&A](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885543707); [Workshop pp2,5](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT10WS01.pdf); [Regional product](https://www.boss.info/fi/products/gt-10/); [Indexed manual p60](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-10_OM.pdf) |

PG-M,PG-F | **I/O routing:** AMP OUT can tap the signal before Cab/IR while the main outs retain cabinet processing. This is **not** a second independent effects chain. Four-cable-method use and external stereo effects insertion are documented. PG-P<br>**Looper / live:** Looper consumes one of the four optional effect slots: **40 seconds mono/20 seconds stereo at full speed;80/40 seconds at half speed**, with one-switch and six-switch implementations documented in the2.50 manual. Yamaha's store comparison mentions Shuffling Looper but the model list/manual used here do not establish it; that remains a source conflict rather than a verified extra algorithm. Tuner,snapshots,trails and USB MIDI are documented; an internal drum machine is not established. Manufacturer describes snapshot changes without audio dropouts or disruption of delay/reverb trails; this is not a measured whole-device latency or blanket preset-switching guarantee. PG-M,PG-Y,PG-P<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** Line 6 specifies **up to30 m/100 ft line-of-sight** and about **seven hours transmitter playing time**, both manufacturer ratings rather than independent tests. The floorboard needs its own supply. RF antennas are above the expression-pedal toe area. Wireless reliability depends on RF conditions; do not cover the antenna or assume arbitrary crowded2.4 GHz conditions meet the rated range. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** GUITAR IN charges the transmitter; the separate rear well stores it. Leaving the transmitter in the charge port while the processor is off slowly discharges it, so the storage well is the documented storage location. Wireless accepts Guitar,Wireless or Guitar+Wireless input selections; ordinary POD Go presets are compatible and their Guitar input is automatically converted to Guitar+Wireless when imported. USB3/4 is a reamp source; USB1/2 is monitoring that bypasses processing. Cable Tone and Wireless Gain are separate settings, not additional audio-effect blocks. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** The [WirelessFAQ](https://kb.line6.com/pod-go-wireless-faq),rechecked2026-10-17,recommends resyncing in each new room: insert the transmitter intoGUITAR IN,wait **10–15seconds**,then reconnect to the guitar. Keep at least **3m** from2.4GHz Wi-Fi routers. Up to **four systems** are described with sequential scanning while earlier transmitters stay awake;after **four minutes** without audio the transmitter sleeps. These are conditional manufacturer instructions,not a venue reliability guarantee or floorboard battery rating. PG-W |

Main outputs are **680Ω impedance-balanced TRS,not true differential balanced**; preserve the manufacturer's distinction. No mic/XLR/phantom,analog aux or FX loop in the complete panel inventory. PE-M,PE-F<br>**POD Express Guitar — routing and software qualifications:** Without headphones,using only L/MONO sums stereo effects to mono. With headphones connected,the manual says L/MONO carries only the left channel for stereo effects; do not promise the same mono-sum behavior in both configurations. Volume expression sits after amp/cab and before post effects. The looper is documented after the processing chain. PE-M<br>**POD Express Guitar — routing and software qualifications:** The2.00 manual has inconsistent OS-minimum tables: its introduction lists macOS11,Windows10,iOS/iPadOS15 and Android6 for editing/8 for audio,while the later editor section lists older macOS10.14 and iOS12.1. Platforms are established; **current minimum supported OS versions remain unresolved**, not selected arbitrarily from one table. ## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 167 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
